# Extraction Atlas

**PURPOSE:** exhaustive, evidence-backed census of the game save paths, per-country schema,
authoritative runtime state, a historical inventory of excluded host-local presentation state,
derived values, and datum-level gaps relevant to replay extraction.
It records *what data exists and what evidence was found*; it is not a completion ledger or an
execution plan. Current completion/residual status lives only in
[`MASTER_EXTRACTION_CONTRACT.md`](MASTER_EXTRACTION_CONTRACT.md), and code-authoritative domain
IDs/status/schedulability live in [`../native/domain_registry.json`](../native/domain_registry.json)
(mirrored by `compact_contract::ASSIGNED_COMPACT_DOMAINS`).

Scope: read-only census generated from the D04 1944.2.11.18 melt, the then-current compact-domain
registry and contract/residual documents, lane/evidence files, and vanilla `hoi4.exe` RTTI/class
strings. No live-memory write or code wiring is part of this document. The generated tables'
`Atlas routing`/feasibility labels are a **2026-07-05 discovery snapshot, superseded as status on
2026-07-14**; retain them as triage provenance only and use the two authoritative sources above
for current status.

**Normative production scope:** STATUS: PROPOSED — owner ratification owed (2026-07-16); aligns with
the standing observer-interaction rulings. Every save-serialized datum and authoritative host-side
simulation fact/event is in scope for semantic coverage. Host-local presentation state is not: camera,
viewport, selections, hover targets, open screens/dialogs, map-mode/layer toggles, outliner state,
tooltips, drag state, and other local UI preferences must not enter production replay data. The
replay interface owns those choices and reconstructs its UI from captured facts. The historical UI
rows in §5 are retained to make the exclusion explicit, not as an RE or fixture backlog.

Generation-time support-file manifest (RECONCILED 2026-07-16): the original
`extractor/native/runs/extraction_atlas_seed/` bundle (including its own
`build_extraction_atlas.py`) is GONE and no archived replacement was found. It is
replaced by an extractor-owned, reproducible generator:
[`../coverage_audit/build_extraction_atlas.py`](../coverage_audit/build_extraction_atlas.py).
Run it to regenerate these outputs into `extractor/coverage_audit/atlas_generated/`:

```
python extractor/coverage_audit/build_extraction_atlas.py            # DEGRADED (tracked data only)
python extractor/coverage_audit/build_extraction_atlas.py --corpus-dir <re_scratch>   # FULL
```

Because the melted-save corpus (`re_scratch/*.txt`) is not tracked in the repo, the
default run is DEGRADED: it regenerates everything derivable from the tracked
key-tree corpus and writes a `<name>.OWED.txt` fail-closed marker (never a
fabricated file) for every output that genuinely needs the melt. Each run emits a
`build_summary.json` reconciling this declared manifest (produced / partial / owed)
and an `atlas_corpus_manifest.json` recording the melt corpus + the four owner-gated
fixture slots. Disposition of the historically-declared outputs:

| declared output | DEGRADED (tracked-data) disposition |
| --- | --- |
| `build_extraction_atlas.py` | **produced** — the generator itself (extractor-owned) |
| `build_summary.json` | **produced** — declared-manifest reconciliation |
| `top_level_save_census.csv` | **produced** — from `save_keytree_union.json` |
| `top_level_save_occurrences.csv` | **produced** — per-scenario from `save_keytree_*.json` |
| `ger_country_depth1_census.csv` | **partial** — normalized all-country depth-1 schema from the tracked recursive walk (GER-specific view needs the melt) |
| `memory_only_ui_ephemeral_census.csv` | **produced** — manifest F/G/H tier rows |
| `residual_fixture_blocked_catalog.csv` | **produced** — parsed from contract registry §A |
| `derived_recompute_catalog.csv` | **produced** — parsed from contract registry §C |
| `save_path_catalog.csv` | **partial** — `countries.*` subtree from the tracked recursive walk; full states/provinces/weather/scripted catalog is melt-gated |
| `ger_country_depth1_entries.csv` | **owed** — GER-specific per-instance occurrences need the melt |
| `dynamic_script_keys.json` | **owed** — concrete script/flag/idea tokens are normalized to `<id>` in tracked data |
| `doc_signal_lines.csv` / `.json` | **owed** — repo doc-tree scan by the absent seed orchestrator |
| `rtti_runtime_class_candidates.csv` | **owed** — vanilla `hoi4.exe` RTTI scan, not save-derived |
| `lane_file_inventory.csv` / `evidence_file_inventory.csv` | **owed** — inventories of the absent seed bundle |
| `compact_contract_evidence.json` | **owed** — superseded; use `native/domain_registry.json` + `compact_contract` |

In FULL mode (a reachable `--corpus-dir`) the four `save_path_catalog` /
`dynamic_script_keys` / `ger_country_depth1_entries` outputs are produced at full
fidelity from the melt; the six repo/exe-scan outputs remain owed (not save-derived).

**OWED — weather deep subtree / open-vocab phenomenon-flag leaf (melt-corpus-gated).** The
exhaustive save-path oracle (`coverage_audit/save_oracle.py`) enumerates NO deep weather path:
the tracked canonical universe stops at `weather.regions` / `weather.provinces`, so an
open-vocab `weather.regions.*.<phenomenon flag>` leaf has no ground truth to validate against.
It is therefore NOT carried as a blanket `weather.regions.*.*` cover — a blanket over that
unenumerated field slot would silently MASK a missing concrete sibling field row (drop the
dedicated `weather.regions.*.temperature` row and the blanket would keep the field "covered").
Instead the open-vocab leaf coverage is surfaced as an **explicit OWED disposition** (an
exhaustive-oracle owed scope, alongside the `states.*` / `provinces.*` deep subtrees), pending
the melt corpus; every CONCRETE weather field keeps its own dedicated manifest row (validated at
spine level). Regenerate with `build_extraction_atlas.py --corpus-dir <melts>` to enumerate the
weather deep subtree and retire this OWED item. (Soundness note: deriving a residual open-vocab
cover that excludes the concrete siblings was rejected — the exclusion set can only be derived
from the present manifest rows, so deleting a concrete row drops it from the set and the residual
cover re-masks it; the only stable alternative is a hardcoded weather-field list, i.e. invented
ground truth, which is not permitted.)

## 0. Generation audit and routing caveat (historical, 2026-07-05)

The D04 enumeration was independently reproduced: 81 unique top-level keys / 134 occurrences and
82,154 canonical save paths. The old registry crosswalk was also reproduced at generation time,
but that crosswalk and all completion assertions are superseded by the 2026-07-14 authorities
linked above.

The per-row `Atlas routing`/feasibility values for loose top-level scalars in §3 are heuristic and
over-apply `GAP-UNINVESTIGATED` / `NEEDS-DISASM`. Treat §3 as exhaustive enumeration, not current
gap classification. Durable corrections/evidence retained from the audit:
- `date` is NOT a gap — it is `u32[CGameState+0x468]`, already extracted as the
  clock/frame infrastructure and used as the same-tick pause gate on every
  validation. (Its `NEEDS-DISASM` label is wrong.)
- `speed` / `pause` — the *pause* bool is cracked (clock singleton `+0x6C1`, see §5)
  and folded via domain 57; only the *speed enum root* tiebreak is genuinely
  FIXTURE-BLOCKED (§5/§7 and the master have this right; §3's `speed` row is optimistic).
- The remaining top-level meta scalars (`version`, `save_version`,
  `minor_save_version`, `difficulty`, `tutorial`, `dlcs`, `game_unique_seed/id`,
  `multiplayer_random_seed/count`, `debug_current_ref_id`, …) are one-read scalars
  off the game-state/session roots, not `NEEDS-DISASM` reverse-engineering targets;
  most are low replay value (determinism/audit metadata) and simply un-wired, not
  hard gaps.
- **Current correction for `game_unique_seed/id`:** W15 hash-bound static RE proves signed
  `i32[CGameState+0x720]` and the MSVC string object at `CGameState+0x728`, and the CID1 codec plus
  recorder-main explicit-mode validator are offline-green. The generated §3 rows do not prove d57
  capture or live identity: the collector reader/barrier and complete live matrix remain open.
- **Current correction for `tech_sharing_group`:** D69 TSG2 v2 pairs each validated country member
  with its same-index signed fixed-5 bonus, carries four authority masks, and fails the whole frame
  on invalid members. `technology_sharing_static` is the seventh required bundle role. Runtime
  intern-table ordinals are sequential and deliberately never hash-guessed or joined to static ids;
  only current-session S9/F6 live acceptance remains open.

Current classification of any named residual must be checked in the master contract.

## 5. Authoritative runtime state and excluded host-local presentation census

STATUS: PROPOSED — owner ratification owed (2026-07-16); aligns with the standing observer-interaction
rulings. This status marker governs the OUT-OF-SCOPE host-local presentation rows below.

These rows are not guaranteed to persist in the save. Authoritative session/runtime facts remain in
scope when they affect or explain the shared multiplayer world. Host-local presentation state is
explicitly out of production scope even if a memory chain could be found; the replay viewer owns its
own camera, selections, hover, panels, and display state. RTTI/class candidates are retained as
historical census evidence only and are not authorization to reverse-engineer excluded UI state.

## 7. Datum-level gap catalog

This preserves the atlas's per-datum gap inventory without becoming a second backlog. The
`Master mapping` column is only a locator into the current
[`MASTER_EXTRACTION_CONTRACT.md`](MASTER_EXTRACTION_CONTRACT.md); that contract alone decides
whether an item is open, blocked, derived, cracked-but-not-wired, or complete.
`doc_signal_lines.csv/json` preserves the generation-time line evidence.

| Name / gap | Master locator (not status) | Fixture/disasm needed | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| hub motorization level/settings | A1 | same-frame hub toggle fixture with at least two known levels, ideally 1 and 3 | [archived wall-crack result](archive/WALL_CRACK_RESULTS_2026-07-04.md); domain 62 |
| focus-paused state | A2 | paused-focus fixture or serializer disasm; D04 has paused=no for all 113 focuses | [archived wall-crack result](archive/WALL_CRACK_RESULTS_2026-07-04.md); domain 45 |
| convoy/port/disrupted supply pressure | A6 | naval-interdiction/disrupted supply fixture with nonzero pressure records | [archived wall-crack result](archive/WALL_CRACK_RESULTS_2026-07-04.md); domain 44 |
| MP chat/messages, hotjoin, tag-switch | A15 | same-build 2-player MP session with controlled join/tag switch and unique chat text | [archived wall-crack result](archive/WALL_CRACK_RESULTS_2026-07-04.md); domain 57 |

## 8. Gap-backlog authority

Removed as a duplicated and now-stale priority queue. Current ordering and disposition live only in
the master contract's OPEN ITEMS REGISTRY; the datum-level census is retained in §7 above.

## 9. Reproducibility notes

- The D04 melt parser canonicalizes dynamic country/state/province/script-token instances, then emits both summary tables and a full `save_path_catalog.csv` for diffable cross-checks.
- The atlas does not claim a memory chain merely from an RTTI/class name. RTTI rows are discovery candidates unless paired with a lane/evidence chain.
- No git command and no cargo command is required by the generation script.
- The live PID was not required for this census; process presence can be checked separately without changing this atlas.
